  • division_remainder_exists · checked inherited prerequisite
    Exact statement in the checked dependency cone
    forall m n. ~(m = 0) -> exists q r. n = m * q + r /\ S r <= m
  • division_remainder_unique · checked inherited prerequisite
    Exact statement in the checked dependency cone
    forall m n q r q2 r2. n = m * q + r -> (exists k. k + S r = m) -> n = m * q2 + r2 -> (exists k. k + S r2 = m) -> q = q2 /\ r = r2
  • divisor_le_nonzero · checked inherited prerequisite
    Exact statement in the checked dependency cone
    forall d n. ~(n = 0) -> (exists q. n = d * q) -> exists k. k + d = n
